([dragonforse/claude-skill-orquestador](https://github.com/dragonforse/claude-skill-orquestador/tree/HEAD/skills/orquestador) · ★1)

Use when the user says "tailor this answer", "rewrite this answer for", "make this answer shorter", "make this answer longer", "make this answer more formal", "adapt the vault answer for", or names an audience an existing answer should be rewritten for. To find the answer first use orrigo-answer-finder; for a whole questionnaire use orrigo-rfp-res… ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-answer-tailor))
- **orrigo-client-deck**: Build or refresh a client presentation from Orrigo's approved content and figures, for the client service and sales teams, whether a quarterly client review pack, a conference presentation or a new business pitch. Use when the user says "build a deck", "build the client review deck", "update last quarter's deck", "refresh the client presentation", "put together slides", or "I need a presentation for". For the figures only use orrigo-update-performance; to prepare for a finals meeting use orrigo-finals-prep; for a number in conversation use orrigo-portfolio-lookup. ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-client-deck))
- **orrigo-consultant-database-update**: Update the firm's profile on an investment consultant's or platform's manager database for a new period, for the consultant relations team, refreshing the firm, strategy, AUM, performance and team fields from Orrigo and showing what changed. Use when the user says "update the consultant database", "refresh our database profile", "update our profile on the consultant's platform", "the database update is due", or names a consultant database that needs this quarter's data. For a recurring questionnaire use orrigo-ddq-update; for a single figure use orrigo-portfolio-lookup. ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-consultant-database-update))
- **orrigo-ddq-update**: Roll a recurring due diligence questionnaire (DDQ) forward to a new period for the firm's RFP team, refreshing every figure from Orrigo, checking every written answer against the approved Vault, and showing what changed. Use when the user says "update the DDQ", "roll the DDQ forward", "refresh last period's questionnaire", "do the semi-annual DDQ", "the quarterly DDQ is due", or names a recurring platform or consultant DDQ that is due. For a new RFP or questionnaire answered for the first time use orrigo-rfp-response; for a consultant database profile use orrigo-consultant-database-update. ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-ddq-update))
- **orrigo-finals-prep**: Prepare the people who will present at a finals meeting with a prospect or its consultant, covering the questions likely to be asked, the firm's approved answer to each, the figures to have ready, and the points to make. Use when the user says "prepare for the finals", "finals prep", "get ready for the finals presentation", "what will they ask at the finals", "prep for the pitch meeting", or names a finals or shortlist meeting that is coming up. To build the slides use orrigo-client-deck; for one approved answer use orrigo-answer-finder. ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-finals-prep))
- **orrigo-newsletter**: Draft the firm's periodic sales-enablement newsletter for its sales team and external distributors, from approved content and Orrigo's figures. Use when the user says "draft the newsletter", "write this month's sales newsletter", "draft the sales update", "what goes in the newsletter", or "the monthly update for the sales team". For a client-facing presentation use orrigo-client-deck; for a single number use orrigo-portfolio-lookup. ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-newsletter))
- **orrigo-portfolio-lookup**: Answer questions about the firm's AUM, net flows, composite performance, attribution, holdings and portfolio characteristics in the conversation, from Orrigo's figures with their as-of dates, for anyone who needs a number. Use when the user says "what is our AUM", "how much do we manage", "how did the strategy perform", "what were the returns in the quarter", "what drove performance", "what are our top holdings", "what is the tracking error", or asks any question answered with a portfolio number. For a presentation use orrigo-client-deck; for a questionnaire use orrigo-rfp-response; for a wri… ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-portfolio-lookup))
- **orrigo-rfp-response**: Draft answers to an institutional RFP, RFI or DDQ being answered for the first time, from the firm's approved answer Vault in Orrigo, for the RFP and sales team to review. Use when the user says "fill the RFP", "answer the RFP", "answer the DDQ", "draft the DDQ", "respond to the questionnaire", "work through the consultant's questions", or asks to continue or finish an RFP already started. For a recurring questionnaire rolled forward from last period use orrigo-ddq-update; for one answer rewritten for an audience use orrigo-answer-tailor; for a single approved answer use orrigo-answer-finder. ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-rfp-response))
- **orrigo-update-performance**: Update the performance and strategy figures in an existing presentation to the latest period from Orrigo, for whoever owns the deck, covering account value and flows, returns against the benchmark, attribution, top holdings and portfolio characteristics, without changing anything else. Use when the user says "update the performance numbers", "update the strategy numbers", "refresh the figures in this deck", "update the performance slides", or "roll the figures forward to the latest quarter". To build or refresh a whole deck use orrigo-client-deck; for a number in conversation use orrigo-portf… ([S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins](https://github.com/S2Strategy/orrigo-agent-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/orrigo-update-performance))
- **orro**: ORRO, the Observed Run & Review Orchestrator, runs evidence-backed repository work through witnessd and Depone. Use when an operator wants to plan, execute, verify, review, hand off, or ship work. Published by Moonweave. ([Moonweave-Systems/witnessd](https://github.com/Moonweave-Systems/witnessd/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **orryx-command-triage**: Daily triage of the Orryx Command dashboard (localhost:4317 / snapshot.json) — plans every needs-you/pending/at-risk/broken/health/work/next-action item into a sprint, and builds an interactive founder decision walk-through so human-gated items never sit as silent blockers. ([alexmclaren/orryx-standards](https://github.com/alexmclaren/orryx-standards/tree/HEAD/routines/prompts/orryx-command-triage))
- **ors-contribution-framing**: Use when articulating the contribution of an Operations Research (OR) manuscript — especially the mandatory cover-letter contribution statement (since 1 June 2023, fewer than 500 words) and the discussion's significance claims. Frames why the work matters to OR; it does not position against specific prior papers (ors-literature-positioning) or run experiments (ors-data-analysis). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-contribution-framing) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **ors-data-analysis**: Use when running and reporting the computational study for an Operations Research (OR) manuscript — benchmark instances, baselines, reproducible experiments, statistical care for stochastic output, and the ORJournal code-and-data reproducibility workflow. Executes and reports the numerical evidence; it does not prove the results (ors-methods) or lay out the exhibits (ors-tables-figures). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-data-analysis) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **ors-literature-positioning**: Use when placing an Operations Research (OR) manuscript against the OR/MS literature — separating your model, results, and algorithmic guarantees from the closest prior work so the novelty is unambiguous. Positions the contribution; it does not formulate the model (ors-theory-development) or write the contribution statement (ors-contribution-framing). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-literature-positioning) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **ors-methods**: Use when designing the proof technique, algorithm, or simulation protocol for an Operations Research (OR) manuscript — choosing the right machinery (duality, dynamic programming, probabilistic coupling, convergence analysis, simulation output analysis) to actually establish the claimed results. Establishes the results; it does not state the model (ors-theory-development) or run the experiments (ors-data-analysis). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-methods) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **ors-rebuttal**: Use when responding to an Operations Research (OR) decision letter — writing the point-by-point response, closing proof gaps, adding baselines/instances, and passing the ORJournal code/data reproducibility review across a revision cycle. Drafts the revision and response; it does not parse the decision in the first place (ors-review-process) or run the final preflight (ors-submission). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-rebuttal) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **ors-review-process**: Use when navigating the Operations Research (OR) editorial process — the departmental area-editor routing, the soft double-anonymous model with asymmetric transparency, decision types, and how to read a decision letter from the handling Area Editor. Explains how review works and how to read a decision; it does not draft the response (ors-rebuttal) or run the preflight (ors-submission). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-review-process) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **ors-submission**: Use when running the final pre-submission preflight for an Operations Research (OR) manuscript — INFORMS Author Portal/ScholarOne, area selection, recommending Associate Editors and reviewers, soft double-anonymous anonymization, the mandatory contribution statement, page-tier limits, and the code/data deposit plan. Checks readiness to submit; it does not handle the post-decision response (ors-rebuttal). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-submission) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **ors-tables-figures**: Use when building the exhibits for an Operations Research (OR) manuscript — theorem/assumption layout, comparison tables against prior work, computational-results tables, and convergence/scaling plots in INFORMS house style. Lays out exhibits; it does not generate the numbers (ors-data-analysis) or prove the results (ors-methods). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-tables-figures) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
- **ors-theory-development**: Use when formulating the model and stating results for an Operations Research (OR) manuscript — defining the optimization/stochastic/simulation model, assumptions, and the theorems, propositions, and lemmas that carry the contribution. Builds the mathematical object and its claimed results; it does not prove them in detail (ors-methods) or run the computational study (ors-data-analysis). ([brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Awesome-Journal-Skills/tree/HEAD/Operations-Research-Skills/skills/ors-theory-development) · ★1.4k · 3 copies)
